All v4 issues have been resolved. This document is the single source of truth.

1.  Project Overview
Echo AI converts meeting audio recordings and transcript files into structured accountability reports. It identifies action items, assigns risk scores, detects status changes, and surfaces items requiring human confirmation.

1.1  What Person 2 Builds
Input Page — two-tab interface: Upload Audio or Upload Transcript File
Audio Upload Flow — audio file sent to POST /api/process-file; transcript returned automatically passed for AI analysis
File Upload Flow — transcript file (.docx / .pdf) sent directly to POST /api/process-file for AI analysis
Dashboard Page — summary stats, high-risk list, donut chart, Smart Confirmation Panel, items table
Smart Confirmation Panel — button-based guided confirmation UI for low-confidence AI status detections
Error display system — every backend error mapped to a user-friendly message via parseApiError()
Loading states — indeterminate progress bar + status-driven messages during processing
Push notification display — FCM alerts with foreground listener when high-risk items are detected

2.  Quick Start Setup

2.1  Install &amp; Run

Scaffold the project
npm create vite@latest echo-ai-frontend -- --template react-ts
cd echo-ai-frontend

# 2. Install all dependencies
npm install react-router-dom axios recharts lucide-react react-hot-toast firebase zustand framer-motion
npm install -D tailwindcss postcss autoprefixer

# 3. Start dev server
npm run dev   # -&gt; http://localhost:5173

3.1  App.tsx
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/App.tsx
import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, useNavigate } from &apos;react-router-dom&apos;;
import InputPage from &apos;./pages/InputPage&apos;;
import DashboardPage from &apos;./pages/DashboardPage&apos;;
import { Toaster } from &apos;react-hot-toast&apos;;
import { useEffect } from &apos;react&apos;;
import { initForegroundListener } from &apos;./services/notifications&apos;;
import { useNotificationStore } from &apos;./stores/notificationStore&apos;;

export default function App() {
  const showBanner = useNotificationStore(s =&gt; s.showBanner);

  useEffect(() =&gt; {
    const unsub = initForegroundListener((n) =&gt; showBanner(n));
    return unsub;  // cleanup on unmount
  }, []);

  return (
    &lt;BrowserRouter&gt;
      &lt;Toaster position=&quot;top-right&quot; /&gt;
      &lt;Routes&gt;
        &lt;Route path=&quot;/&quot; element={&lt;InputPage /&gt;} /&gt;
        &lt;Route path=&quot;/dashboard&quot; element={&lt;DashboardPage /&gt;} /&gt;
      &lt;/Routes&gt;
    &lt;/BrowserRouter&gt;
  );
}

4.2  Risk Badge Specification
RiskBadge.tsx accepts two props: risk (string) and score (number). API values are lowercase. The component derives the display tier.

5.  Data Layer
5.1  Meeting ID Generation
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="FDCB6E" w:sz="8"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="1A1500" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="80"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="80"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:i/><w:iCs/><w:color w:val="FDCB6E"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">?? FIXED (v4 bug):  Date.now() alone is not unique across tabs, users, or distributed systems. v5 uses crypto.randomUUID().

<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/utils/ids.ts
export function generateMeetingId(): string {
  // crypto.randomUUID() — collision-proof, UUID v4
  if (typeof crypto !== &apos;undefined&apos; &amp;&amp; crypto.randomUUID) {
    return `mtg_${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
  }
  // Fallback for older environments
  return `mtg_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`;
}

5.2  Type Definitions
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/types/upload.ts
export type UploadStatus =
  | &apos;idle&apos;          // waiting for user input
  | &apos;transcribing&apos;  // file dispatched; processing
  | &apos;analyzing&apos;     // NLP pipeline running
  | &apos;success&apos;       // meetingData available
  | &apos;error&apos;;        // terminal; user must retry

// Allowed transitions only:
// idle ? transcribing ? analyzing ? success
//                                 ? error
// error ? idle  (user-initiated reset)

<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/types/meeting.ts
export interface MeetingMeta {
  title:        string;
  date:         string;  // ISO 8601
  participants: string[];
}

export interface ActionItem {
  id:                string;
  task:              string;
  owner:             string;
  status:            string;
  due_date:          string;
  risk_keywords:     string[];
  evidence:          string;
  score:             number;
  risk:              &apos;high&apos; | &apos;medium&apos; | &apos;low&apos;;
  reason:            string;
  confidence:        number;
  needs_confirmation: boolean;
}

export interface MeetingData {
  id:          string;
  meta:        MeetingMeta;   // ? was missing in v4
  transcript:  string;
  actionItems: ActionItem[];
  summary:     string;
  analytics:   Analytics;
  createdAt:   string;
}

5.4  Data Normalization Layer
All API responses pass through a single transform before entering the Zustand store. If the backend shape changes, only this file needs updating.

<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/api/normalize.ts
import { MeetingData, MeetingMeta } from &apos;@/types/meeting&apos;;

export function transformMeetingData(
  raw: unknown,
  meta: MeetingMeta
): MeetingData {
  const r = raw as Record&lt;string, unknown&gt;;
  return {
    id:          String(r.meeting_id ?? generateMeetingId()),
    meta,
    transcript:  String(r.transcript ?? &apos;&apos;),
    actionItems: Array.isArray(r.items) ? r.items.map(normalizeItem) : [],
    summary:     String(r.summary ?? &apos;&apos;),
    analytics:   normalizeAnalytics(r),
    createdAt:   new Date().toISOString(),
  };
}

5.5  Storage Strategy
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="FDCB6E" w:sz="8"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="1A1500" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="80"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="80"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:i/><w:iCs/><w:color w:val="FDCB6E"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">?? FIXED (v4 bug):  v4 used sessionStorage while the UI behaved as a persistent workspace. v5 uses localStorage with a versioned key and graceful fallback.

<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/utils/storage.ts
const STORAGE_KEY = &apos;echoai_v5_meetings&apos;;

export function saveMeetings(data: MeetingData[]): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
  } catch {
    // QuotaExceededError — silent fail; data still in memory
  }
}

export function loadMeetings(): MeetingData[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as MeetingData[]) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

6.4  Error Handling — parseApiError()
All API errors are normalized to a single shape before reaching components. Components never inspect raw HTTP errors.

<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/api/errors.ts
export type ApiErrorCode =
  | &apos;FILE_TOO_LARGE&apos;   | &apos;INVALID_FILE_TYPE&apos;
  | &apos;QUOTA_EXCEEDED&apos;   | &apos;PROCESSING_FAILED&apos;
  | &apos;NETWORK_ERROR&apos;    | &apos;TIMEOUT&apos;
  | &apos;UNKNOWN&apos;;

export interface AppError {
  code:    ApiErrorCode;
  message: string;
  retry:   boolean;
}

export function parseApiError(err: unknown): AppError {
  if (err instanceof Response) {
    if (err.status === 413) return { code: &apos;FILE_TOO_LARGE&apos;,    message: &apos;File exceeds 100 MB limit.&apos;,         retry: false };
    if (err.status === 415) return { code: &apos;INVALID_FILE_TYPE&apos;, message: &apos;Unsupported format.&apos;,                retry: false };
    if (err.status === 429) return { code: &apos;QUOTA_EXCEEDED&apos;,    message: &apos;Too many requests — wait a moment.&apos;, retry: true  };
    if (err.status &gt;= 500)  return { code: &apos;PROCESSING_FAILED&apos;, message: &apos;Server error — retrying…&apos;,           retry: true  };
  }
  if (err instanceof TypeError) return { code: &apos;NETWORK_ERROR&apos;, message: &apos;No connection.&apos;,   retry: true };
  return { code: &apos;UNKNOWN&apos;, message: &apos;Something went wrong.&apos;, retry: true };
}

6.5  Automatic Retry Strategy
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="FDCB6E" w:sz="8"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="1A1500" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="80"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="80"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:i/><w:iCs/><w:color w:val="FDCB6E"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">?? FIXED (v4 bug):  v4 had no retry logic — any network hiccup required a full manual retry. v5 adds exponential back-off for retryable errors.

<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/api/retry.ts
export async function withRetry&lt;T&gt;(
  fn: () =&gt; Promise&lt;T&gt;,
  maxAttempts = 3,
  baseDelayMs = 800,
): Promise&lt;T&gt; {
  let attempt = 0;
  while (true) {
    try {
      return await fn();
    } catch (err) {
      const parsed = parseApiError(err);
      attempt++;
      if (!parsed.retry || attempt &gt;= maxAttempts) throw err;
      await new Promise(r =&gt; setTimeout(r, baseDelayMs * 2 ** (attempt - 1)));
    }
  }
}

// Usage:
await withRetry(() =&gt; api.updateStatus(itemId, newStatus));

7.  File Handling
7.1  Validation — Type + Extension Fallback
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="FDCB6E" w:sz="8"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="1A1500" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="80"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="80"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:i/><w:iCs/><w:color w:val="FDCB6E"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">?? FIXED (v4 bug):  .docx and .pdf files often arrive as application/octet-stream, causing valid files to be rejected when validated by MIME type alone. v5 uses dual MIME + extension check.

<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/utils/fileValidation.ts
const ALLOWED_MIME = new Set([
  &apos;audio/mpeg&apos;, &apos;audio/wav&apos;, &apos;audio/mp4&apos;, &apos;audio/ogg&apos;,
  &apos;application/pdf&apos;,
  &apos;application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document&apos;,
  &apos;application/octet-stream&apos;,  // ? fallback for misidentified files
]);

const ALLOWED_EXT = new Set([
  &apos;.mp3&apos;, &apos;.wav&apos;, &apos;.m4a&apos;, &apos;.ogg&apos;,
  &apos;.pdf&apos;, &apos;.docx&apos;,
]);

const MAX_SIZE_MB = 100;

export interface ValidationResult {
  valid:   boolean;
  reason?: string;
}

export function validateFile(file: File): ValidationResult {
  const ext = &apos;.&apos; + file.name.split(&apos;.&apos;).pop()?.toLowerCase();
  const mimeOk = ALLOWED_MIME.has(file.type);
  const extOk  = ALLOWED_EXT.has(ext);

  if (!mimeOk &amp;&amp; !extOk)
    return { valid: false, reason: `Unsupported file type (${file.type || ext}).` };

  if (file.size &gt; MAX_SIZE_MB * 1024 * 1024)
    return { valid: false, reason: `File too large. Max size is ${MAX_SIZE_MB} MB.` };

  return { valid: true };
}

7.2  Upload Flow (hooks/useUpload.ts)
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">async function handleUpload(file: File): Promise&lt;void&gt; {
  const validation = validateFile(file);
  if (!validation.valid) {
    toast.error(validation.reason);
    return;
  }

  const meetingId = generateMeetingId();   // crypto.randomUUID()-based
  setStatus(&apos;transcribing&apos;);

  try {
    const raw  = await withRetry(() =&gt; api.processFile(file, meetingId));
    const data = transformMeetingData(raw, { title, date, participants });
    setMeetingData(data);
    saveMeetings([...loadMeetings(), data]);  // persist to localStorage
    setStatus(&apos;success&apos;);
    toast.success(`Analysis complete! ${data.actionItems.length} items found.`);
    setTimeout(() =&gt; navigate(&apos;/dashboard&apos;), 1500);
  } catch (err) {
    const appErr = parseApiError(err);
    toast.error(appErr.message);
    setStatus(&apos;error&apos;);
  }
}

8.3  UploadDropzone Component  (src/components/UploadDropzone.tsx)
Single shared dropzone component used for both upload modes. Props control mode-specific behavior.

<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/components/UploadDropzone.tsx
interface Props {
  mode: &apos;audio&apos; | &apos;transcript&apos;;
  file: File | null;
  setFile: (f: File | null) =&gt; void;
  disabled: boolean;
}

const CONFIG = {
  audio:      { accept: &apos;.mp3,.mp4,.wav,.m4a,.ogg&apos;, icon: Mic,    label: &apos;Drop an audio file here, or click to browse&apos;, hint: &apos;Supported: .mp3  .mp4  .wav  .m4a  .ogg  —  Max 100 MB&apos; },
  transcript: { accept: &apos;.docx,.pdf&apos;,               icon: Upload, label: &apos;Drop a transcript file here, or click to browse&apos;, hint: &apos;Supported: .docx  .pdf  —  Max 100 MB&apos; },
};

export default function UploadDropzone({ mode, file, setFile, disabled }: Props) {
  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);
  const [validationError, setValidationError] = useState&lt;string | null&gt;(null);
  const inputRef = useRef&lt;HTMLInputElement&gt;(null);
  const { icon: Icon, accept, label, hint } = CONFIG[mode];

  function handleFile(f: File) {
    setValidationError(null);
    const result = validateFile(f);  // dual MIME + extension check
    if (!result.valid) { setValidationError(result.reason ?? &apos;Invalid file&apos;); return; }
    setFile(f);
  }

  return (
    &lt;div
      onDragOver={e =&gt; { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(true); }}
      onDragLeave={() =&gt; setDragOver(false)}
      onDrop={e =&gt; { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(false); const f = e.dataTransfer.files[0]; if (f) handleFile(f); }}
      onClick={() =&gt; !disabled &amp;&amp; inputRef.current?.click()}
      className={`border-2 border-dashed rounded-xl p-8 text-center cursor-pointer
        transition-all duration-200
        ${dragOver ? &apos;ring-2 ring-[#00D4FF] border-[#00D4FF]&apos; : &apos;border-[#533483]&apos;}
        ${disabled ? &apos;opacity-50 pointer-events-none&apos; : &apos;hover:border-[#00D4FF]/60&apos;}`}
    &gt;
      &lt;input ref={inputRef} type=&quot;file&quot; className=&quot;hidden&quot; accept={accept}
        onChange={e =&gt; { if (e.target.files?.[0]) handleFile(e.target.files[0]); }} /&gt;
      {file ? (
        &lt;div className=&quot;flex items-center justify-between px-2&quot;&gt;
          &lt;div className=&quot;flex items-center gap-3&quot;&gt;
            &lt;Icon className=&quot;w-6 h-6 text-[#00D4FF] flex-shrink-0&quot; /&gt;
            &lt;div className=&quot;text-left&quot;&gt;
              &lt;p className=&quot;text-gray-200 text-sm font-medium&quot;&gt;{file.name}&lt;/p&gt;
              &lt;p className=&quot;text-gray-500 text-xs&quot;&gt;{(file.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB&lt;/p&gt;
            &lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;/div&gt;
          &lt;button onClick={e =&gt; { e.stopPropagation(); setFile(null); setValidationError(null); }}
            className=&quot;text-gray-500 hover:text-red-400 transition-colors p-1&quot;&gt;
            &lt;X className=&quot;w-5 h-5&quot; /&gt;
          &lt;/button&gt;
        &lt;/div&gt;
      ) : (
        &lt;&gt;
          &lt;Icon className=&quot;w-8 h-8 text-[#00D4FF] mx-auto mb-3&quot; /&gt;
          &lt;p className=&quot;text-gray-300 text-sm mb-1&quot;&gt;{label}&lt;/p&gt;
          &lt;p className=&quot;text-gray-500 text-xs&quot;&gt;{hint}&lt;/p&gt;
        &lt;/&gt;
      )}
      {validationError &amp;&amp; &lt;p className=&quot;text-red-400 text-xs mt-3&quot;&gt;{validationError}&lt;/p&gt;}
    &lt;/div&gt;
  );
}

8.4  Metadata Row
Three fields below the dropzone, horizontal on desktop, stacked on mobile
Meeting Title — text input, placeholder &quot;e.g. Q3 Sprint Review&quot;
Date — date input, default to today&apos;s date via todayISO() helper
Participants — text input, placeholder &quot;Alice, Bob, Charlie&quot; (stored as string[], split on comma)
All inputs: bg-[#0F3460]/60 border border-[#533483]/40 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-gray-200
All inputs disabled while status === &quot;transcribing&quot; or &quot;analyzing&quot;

8.6  InputPage Component Outline (src/pages/InputPage.tsx)
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/pages/InputPage.tsx
export default function InputPage() {
  const [mode, setMode]       = useState&lt;&apos;audio&apos; | &apos;transcript&apos;&gt;(&apos;audio&apos;);
  const [audioFile, setAudioFile]         = useState&lt;File | null&gt;(null);
  const [transcriptFile, setTranscriptFile] = useState&lt;File | null&gt;(null);
  const [title, setTitle]     = useState(&apos;&apos;);
  const [date, setDate]       = useState(todayISO());
  const [participants, setParticipants] = useState(&apos;&apos;);
  const [status, setStatus]   = useState&lt;UploadStatus&gt;(&apos;idle&apos;);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  function switchMode(newMode: &apos;audio&apos; | &apos;transcript&apos;) {
    setMode(newMode);
    if (newMode === &apos;audio&apos;)      setTranscriptFile(null);
    if (newMode === &apos;transcript&apos;) setAudioFile(null);
  }

  const currentFile = mode === &apos;audio&apos; ? audioFile : transcriptFile;
  const setCurrentFile = mode === &apos;audio&apos; ? setAudioFile : setTranscriptFile;

  const isDisabled = status === &apos;transcribing&apos; || status === &apos;analyzing&apos; || !currentFile;
}

FIXED (v4 bug):  v4 showed a static 90%-wide bar that never reached 100% and was not tied to real progress, confusing users into thinking the app was frozen. v5 uses an indeterminate animation.

<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/components/ProgressBar.tsx
interface Props { status: UploadStatus; }

export function ProgressBar({ status }: Props) {
  if (status === &apos;idle&apos; || status === &apos;success&apos;) return null;

  return (
    &lt;div className=&quot;w-full h-1.5 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden&quot;&gt;
      {/* Indeterminate — loops until status resolves */}
      &lt;div className=&quot;h-full bg-[#00D4FF] rounded-full animate-indeterminate&quot; /&gt;
    &lt;/div&gt;
  );
}

// tailwind.config.ts — add keyframe:
// &apos;indeterminate&apos;: {
//   &apos;0%&apos;:   { transform: &apos;translateX(-100%) scaleX(0.5)&apos; },
//   &apos;100%&apos;: { transform: &apos;translateX(200%)  scaleX(0.5)&apos; }
// }

10.3  Zone B — High Risk Items List
Title: &quot;High Risk Items&quot; + AlertTriangle icon (text-red-400)
Items where risk === &quot;high&quot;, sorted by score descending, max 5 shown
Each row: [RiskBadge] [task truncated 70 chars] [owner] [due_date]
Clickable row: sets selectedItemId in store, scrolls Items Table to that row
Empty state: &quot;No high risk items detected.&quot; in text-gray-500
Card: bg-[#16213E] border border-red-900/30 rounded-xl p-4

10.4  Zone C — Risk Distribution Chart
Recharts PieChart with innerRadius={60} outerRadius={90} (donut, NOT a full pie)
Segments: high (red #D63031), medium (amber #FDCB6E), low (green #00B894)
Custom HTML legend: colored square + label + count
Card: bg-[#16213E] border border-[#533483]/30 rounded-xl p-4

10.5  Zone D — Smart Confirmation Panel
The centerpiece feature. Displayed only when one or more items have needs_confirmation === true. When all items are confirmed it disappears automatically. This is a guided confirmation system — button-based, not a chatbot.

Panel Container
Visible condition: actionItems.filter(i =&gt; i.needs_confirmation).length &gt; 0
Card: bg-[#16213E] border-2 border-[#533483]/60 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden
Panel header (bg-[#0F3460] px-6 py-4):
  Left: Clock icon (cyan 18px) + &quot;Action Required&quot; (white, font-semibold) + badge: count of unconfirmed items
  Right: &quot;X of Y confirmed&quot; progress text in text-gray-400 text-sm
Scrollable body: max-h-[480px] overflow-y-auto px-4 py-3 space-y-3

const [removingIds, setRemovingIds] = useState&lt;Set&lt;string&gt;&gt;(new Set());

function dismissItem(id: string) {
  // 1. Mark as &apos;removing&apos; — triggers CSS exit animation
  setRemovingIds(prev =&gt; new Set(prev).add(id));

  // 2. Wait for animation (300 ms) THEN remove from DOM
  setTimeout(() =&gt; {
    setRemovingIds(prev =&gt; { const next = new Set(prev); next.delete(id); return next; });
    removeConfirmItem(id);   // update store
  }, 300);
}

// Render each item card:
// className={`transition-all duration-300 ${removingIds.has(item.id)
//   ? &apos;opacity-0 scale-95 pointer-events-none&apos;
//   : &apos;opacity-100 scale-100&apos;}`}

Per-Item Card (chat-bubble style)
Card: bg-[#0D1B2A] rounded-xl p-4 border border-[#533483]/30
Row 1 — AI label: small bot icon (12px, cyan) + &quot;Echo AI detected a status update&quot; (text-gray-400 text-xs)
Row 2 — Task text: full task, no truncation, text-white text-sm font-medium, mt-2
Row 3 — Evidence quote: left-quote icon + italic text-gray-400 text-sm, mt-1
Row 4 — Info chips: Detected status chip + Confidence badge (&lt; 50% ? red | 50–74% ? yellow | = 75% ? green)
Row 5 — Confirmation buttons: three buttons, stacked on mobile, inline on desktop, mt-3

Button Behaviour
On click: call api.updateStatus via PATCH /api/meeting/:id/status
While loading: all 3 buttons disabled. Only the clicked button shows a Loader2 spinner.
On success: call dismissItem(id) which triggers animation then removes from store. Show success toast.
On error: toast.error(parseApiError(err).message). All buttons re-enable. Item stays in panel.
When last item confirmed: entire panel fades out and unmounts. &quot;Need Review&quot; stat drops to 0.

Behaviour
Default sort: score descending
Click column header to sort ascending / descending — show arrow icon on active sort column
Filter bar: Risk dropdown (all/high/medium/low) + Status dropdown + Owner text search + Clear Filters button
Pagination: 10 rows per page, Previous/Next buttons, &quot;Page 1 of 3&quot; indicator
Highlighted row: selectedItemId match ? ring-2 ring-[#00D4FF], scrollIntoView()
Score cell: &gt;= 90 ? text-red-400 | &gt;= 70 ? text-orange-400 | &gt;= 40 ? text-yellow-400 | else text-green-400
Owner &quot;unknown&quot; ? show &quot;— unassigned —&quot; in text-gray-500
Due date &quot;unspecified&quot; ? show &quot;—&quot; in text-gray-500
CSV export button: downloads all items (unfiltered) using exportToCsv() utility
Empty state: &quot;No items match your filters.&quot; centred with Clear Filters button

const [page,         setPage]         = useState(1);
const [ownerQuery,   setOwnerQuery]   = useState(&apos;&apos;);
const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState&lt;string&gt;(&apos;all&apos;);

// Debounce text input — prevents re-render on every keystroke
const debouncedQuery = useDebounce(ownerQuery, 300);

// CRITICAL: reset to page 1 whenever filters change
useEffect(() =&gt; { setPage(1); }, [debouncedQuery, statusFilter]);

function useDebounce&lt;T&gt;(value: T, delay: number): T {
  const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState(value);
  useEffect(() =&gt; {
    const t = setTimeout(() =&gt; setDebounced(value), delay);
    return () =&gt; clearTimeout(t);
  }, [value, delay]);
  return debounced;
}

11.  CSV Export
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="FDCB6E" w:sz="8"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="1A1500" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="80"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="80"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:i/><w:iCs/><w:color w:val="FDCB6E"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">?? FIXED (v4 bug):  v4 omitted the UTF-8 BOM, causing Excel on Windows to mis-parse non-ASCII characters (accented names, CJK, emoji in titles).

<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/utils/exportCsv.ts
export function exportToCsv(items: ActionItem[], filename = &apos;echoai_export.csv&apos;): void {
  const header = [&apos;ID&apos;, &apos;Task&apos;, &apos;Owner&apos;, &apos;Due Date&apos;, &apos;Score&apos;, &apos;Risk&apos;, &apos;Status&apos;, &apos;Confidence&apos;];

  const rows = items.map(i =&gt; [
    i.id, i.task, i.owner, i.due_date,
    i.score.toString(), i.risk, i.status,
    Math.round(i.confidence * 100) + &apos;%&apos;,
  ]);

  const csvContent = [header, ...rows]
    .map(row =&gt; row.map(cell =&gt; `&quot;${String(cell).replace(/&quot;/g, &apos;\&quot;&apos;)}&quot;`).join(&apos;,&apos;))
    .join(&apos;\n&apos;);

  // ? UTF-8 BOM prevents Excel from misreading non-ASCII
  const blob = new Blob([&apos;\uFEFF&apos; + csvContent], { type: &apos;text/csv;charset=utf-8;&apos; });
  const url  = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a    = Object.assign(document.createElement(&apos;a&apos;), { href: url, download: filename });
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

12.  Push Notifications (FCM)
12.1  Service Worker  (public/firebase-messaging-sw.js)

12.2  Permission Flow &amp; Token Registration
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/services/notifications.ts
import { getMessaging, getToken, onMessage } from &apos;firebase/messaging&apos;;

// 1. Request permission + register token
export async function registerPushNotifications(userId: string): Promise&lt;void&gt; {
  const permission = await Notification.requestPermission();
  if (permission !== &apos;granted&apos;) return;

  const messaging = getMessaging();
  const token = await getToken(messaging, { vapidKey: VAPID_KEY });
  await api.registerFcmToken({ token, userId });
}

12.3  Foreground Message Listener
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="FDCB6E" w:sz="8"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="1A1500" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="80"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="80"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:i/><w:iCs/><w:color w:val="FDCB6E"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">?? FIXED (v4 bug):  v4 covered token registration and backend relay but omitted the foreground message listener — NotificationBanner had no trigger when the app was open.

<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// 2. Foreground listener — fires when app IS open (was missing in v4)
export function initForegroundListener(
  onNotification: (payload: NotificationPayload) =&gt; void
): () =&gt; void {
  const messaging = getMessaging();
  const unsubscribe = onMessage(messaging, (payload) =&gt; {
    onNotification({
      title:     payload.notification?.title ?? &apos;EchoAI&apos;,
      body:      payload.notification?.body  ?? &apos;&apos;,
      meetingId: payload.data?.meetingId,
    });
  });
  return unsubscribe;   // call in useEffect cleanup
}

// 3. Mount in App.tsx:
useEffect(() =&gt; {
  const unsub = initForegroundListener((n) =&gt; showNotificationBanner(n));
  return unsub;
}, []);

12.4  NotificationBanner (foreground alerts)
Position: fixed top-4 right-4 z-50 w-80
Background: bg-[#D63031] rounded-xl p-4 shadow-xl
AlertTriangle icon + &quot;High Risk Alert:&quot; + task text
&quot;View Item&quot; button: sets selectedItemId in store, scrolls to row in table
Auto-dismiss after 8 seconds. X button for manual dismiss.

<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="334466" w:sz="1"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:left w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:bottom w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/><w:right w:val="none" w:color="FFFFFF" w:sz="0"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="0A1628" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="20" w:before="20"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Courier New" w:cs="Courier New" w:eastAsia="Courier New" w:hAnsi="Courier New"/><w:color w:val="7DD3FC"/><w:sz w:val="18"/><w:szCs w:val="18"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">// src/main.tsx
import React from &apos;react&apos;;
import ReactDOM from &apos;react-dom/client&apos;;
import App from &apos;./App&apos;;
import &apos;./index.css&apos;;

if (&apos;serviceWorker&apos; in navigator)
  navigator.serviceWorker.register(&apos;/firebase-messaging-sw.js&apos;)
    .catch(e =&gt; console.warn(&apos;SW failed:&apos;, e));

ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById(&apos;root&apos;)!)
  .render(&lt;React.StrictMode&gt;&lt;App /&gt;&lt;/React.StrictMode&gt;);

Phase 1 — Foundation &amp; Mock Mode
Project Setup
Vite + React + TypeScript project created, Tailwind configured with custom colors and indeterminate keyframe
.env created with VITE_API_URL=http://localhost:8000 and VITE_USE_MOCK=true
App.tsx: BrowserRouter + Routes + Toaster + foreground FCM listener useEffect
All folders and files per Section 2.3 structure

Data Layer
mockData.ts: 10 items, all field names match Section 6.2, varied risk/status/confidence/needs_confirmation
api/adapter.ts: USE_MOCK flag routing mockApi vs realApi
utils/ids.ts: generateMeetingId() using crypto.randomUUID() with fallback
utils/fileValidation.ts: dual MIME + extension check, 100 MB limit
api/errors.ts: parseApiError() mapping all 5 error codes to AppError
api/retry.ts: withRetry() with exponential back-off, 3 attempts
api/normalize.ts: transformMeetingData() with embedded MeetingMeta
utils/storage.ts: localStorage with versioned key &apos;echoai_v5_meetings&apos;, silent QuotaExceeded fail
utils/exportCsv.ts: UTF-8 BOM prepended, double-quote escaping

Input Page
Exactly two tabs: &quot;Upload Audio&quot; and &quot;Upload Transcript&quot; — no text paste tab
Switching tabs clears the file in the other tab
UploadDropzone.tsx: shared component, mode prop, drag-and-drop, dual validation, filename/size, clear button, disabled state
Metadata row: title, date (today default), participants — disabled while processing
Submit button: correct label + icon per active tab, disabled logic correct
All 4 status states: idle / transcribing / analyzing / success / error
ProgressBar: indeterminate animation, not static 90%
StatusMessage: driven by status state, not hardcoded setTimeout
handleUpload: validate ? generateId ? withRetry(processFile) ? transformMeetingData ? saveMeetings ? navigate
Success: toast + navigate to /dashboard after 1500ms
Error: parseApiError toast, all inputs re-enabled, status back to &quot;error&quot;

Dashboard
Guard: redirect to / if meetingData is null
Summary stats bar: 5 blocks, correct data sources per Section 10.2
High Risk List: top 5 by score, clickable rows, empty state
Risk Chart: PieChart innerRadius={60}, custom HTML legend
EmptyState: shown when meetings.length === 0
Smart Confirmation Panel: visible only when needs_confirmation items exist
Panel: removingIds state with 300ms unmount delay for exit animation
Per-item card: AI label row, task, evidence quote, status chip, confidence badge, 3 buttons
Buttons: correct labels, API status values, per-button spinner, animate-out on success, re-enable on error
Panel auto-hides when all items confirmed; &quot;Need Review&quot; stat drops to 0
Items Table: 7 columns, sort, filter (risk/status/owner with debounce), pagination 10/page
Filters: page resets to 1 on every filter change
Items Table: highlighted + scrolled row when selectedItemId matches
Items Table: CSV export with UTF-8 BOM
Items Table: mobile card-per-row with data-label attributes
Dashboard skeleton while loading
Notification permission banner on first visit

Error Handling
All 10 error scenarios from Section 13 produce correct user messages
No raw error.message or error.response.data ever shown to users
withRetry() covers retryable errors before surfacing to UI

Responsiveness
Mobile 375px: hamburger, stats scroll, card-per-row table, stacked confirm buttons
Tablet 768px: 2-column layout
Desktop 1280px: full 3-column layout

Phase 2 — Live API Switch
VITE_API_URL updated to ngrok URL from Person 4
VITE_USE_MOCK set to false in .env
POST /api/process-file tested with .mp3, .wav, .m4a, .docx, .pdf files
Full pipeline tested: upload ? analysis ? dashboard display
PATCH /api/meeting/:id/status tested: item removed from Smart Confirmation Panel
VITE_FIREBASE_* variables filled in from Person 3
FCM token registration tested: POST /api/notifications/register
Foreground notification banner shown when app is open
Push notification received and banner shown when app is in background
Full end-to-end (both modes): Input ? Process ? Dashboard ? Confirm Panel ? Confirm
Unit tests passing: validateFile, parseApiError, withRetry, generateMeetingId

17.  AI Coding Agent — Build Sequence
Paste the preamble first, then each phase in order. Do not skip or combine phases.

19.  Collaboration Rules
Never import from another person&apos;s module. All data flows through api/adapter.ts.
Never hardcode the API URL. Always use import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL.
Keep VITE_USE_MOCK=true in .env for all committed code. Switch locally only.
API field names are snake_case. TypeScript types use camelCase. Never mix in components.
Status display labels use Title Case. API status values are snake_case lowercase.
Risk API values are lowercase (&quot;high&quot;/&quot;medium&quot;/&quot;low&quot;). Badge display is uppercase (CRITICAL/HIGH/etc).
All user-visible errors route through parseApiError(). Never show raw error.message or response data.
The /dashboard route must redirect to / if meetingData is null.
SmartConfirmPanel is buttons-only. No text input, no conversational AI.
No TypeScript &apos;any&apos; casts in components. Use proper types from src/types/.
PascalCase for components (SmartConfirmPanel.tsx). camelCase for utilities (helpers.ts).
Person 4 provides ngrok URL: update .env only. Zero code changes.
Person 3 provides Firebase config: fill firebase-messaging-sw.js + .env. Zero component changes.
